Rust 1.99.1 standard libraries and runtime notices
Source: the installed toolchain's official COPYRIGHT.html and license files.
This includes dependency families present in the iOS target sysroot, including build/debug
and platform variants. Inclusion does not assert every listed version is linked into FTA.
No implementation source is included. Original license/notice text is preserved.
